1b1(rgyud): @##//rgya gar skad du/a nA bi la tan+t+ra rA dzA nA ma/bod skad du/rgyud 
1b2(rgyud): kyi rgyal po rnyog pa med pa zhes bya ba/rdo rje mkha' 'gro la phyag 'tshal lo//sems 
1b3(rgyud): can rnams la phan 'dod pas//rnyog med rab tu bshad par bya//snang ba med 
1b4(rgyud): cing dpe med pa//tshig gi yul las rnam par 'das//zhi zhing bdag med 
2a1(rgyud): @##//dag pa nyid//mtshan med mtshan gzhi rnam par spangs//ngang gis sangs rgyas 'di nyid 
2a2(rgyud): tshul||mtshan med mtshan gzhi rnams par spangs//khyad par du yang mdor bsdus pas//bdag gis gsal 
2a3(rgyud): bar bshad pa yin//rmongs pa rnams la phan 'dod pas//snying rjes 'di ni bstan pa yin//kha cig stong 
2a4(rgyud): par 'dod pa dang //kha cig thig le'i gzugs can dang //kha cig khyab 'jug tshul gyis ni//blo dman 
2a5(rgyud): dag ni 'jog par byed//de nyid nges par yang dag par//rgyu yi gnas kyi spyod yul nyid//rgyu dang 'bras bu 
2b1(rgyud): ma grub na//bsgoms pa med cing byang chub med//rgyu yi yid ches spangs pa 'di//thog ma tha ma med pa'i bdag/sgyu ma'i ye 
2b2(rgyud): shes las byung ba//rnam pa sna tshogs ston par byed//kha cig drag po'i rang bzhin du//kha cig zhi ba chen po che//kha 
2b3(rgyud): cig phyag rgya'i rang bzhin gzugs//kha cig 'dar zhing g.yo ba dang //glu gar de bzhin sel snyan dang //lha yi sngags ni brjod 
2b4(rgyud): pa nyid//kha cig sngags ni yang dag dgod//kha cig dkyil 'khor bri ba dang //dbang la sogs pa brtag pa dang //'byung dang 
2b5(rgyud): 'byung las gyur pa'i lus//de ltar la sogs mtha' yas mchog/sangs rgyas la sogs rdo rje brtags//gang tshe sems 
2b6(rgyud): ni legs gnas pa//de bzhin nyid kyi spyod yul nyid//dkyil 'khor de bzhin 'gyur ba ni//phan 'dod rnams kyis shes 
3a1(rgyud): @##//par bya//phung po la sogs tshogs rnams kyis//gang zhig khyad par gyis bcos pa//lus ngag yid 
3a2(rgyud): gsum bcos pa'i phyir//rdul tshon dkyil 'khor zhes byar gsungs//phung sogs gzhan sogs mthong ba'i phyir//tshogs kyi 
3a3(rgyud): dkyil 'khor zhes byar gsung //sems kyi dkyil 'khor dag pa ste//rang bzhin med par bsgoms par bya//me long lta bu'i 
3a4(rgyud): gcig nyid de//dang po grur ni rtogs par bya//so sor rtog pa zhes gsungs pas//shar gyi grur ni rnam par rgyan//bya ba nan 
3a5(rgyud): tan khyad 'phags pa//byang gi zur ni rnam par spros//mnyam nyid ye shes chen po ni//bag chags med gru rjes rtogs byed//
3a6(rgyud): gzhan du'ang dkyil 'khor gru bzhi pa//grwa yi dbus ni thams cad du//de nyid 'di ni rang 'jug pas//ye shes spyod yul du 
3b1(rgyud): ni brtag/sgo bzhir yang dag brjod pa ni//de bzhin dran pa bzhir byung ba//de bzhin gra bzhir gsungs pa ni//sbyin pa bzhi ni rnam dag pa//dbus 
3b2(rgyud): su dkyil 'khor gzugs ni//mi bskyod pa yi rnam par rgyan//zhi dang chos don rgyan pa las//de dag thams cad dkyil 'khor 'grub//dra ba dra byed 
3b3(rgyud): zla ba sogs//dkyil 'khor du ni bris pa dang //rta babs la sogs thams cad ni//ting 'dzin yan lag rta babs nyid//sems kyi mthar thug brjod 
3b4(rgyud): pa ste||de nyid rang bzhin las ni gsungs//gang zhig sems kyi rab 'phel ba//ting 'dzin yan lag rnam par gnas//de nyid dra ba zhes su gsungs//
3b5(rgyud): da ba phyed pa phyed du ste//bri ba bu do shal brtag pa ni//rang gi btsun mo rtogs par byed//sgra brnyan rnam pa lta bur ni//chos rnams thams cad so sor 
3b6(rgyud): rtogs//chos rnams ma skyes mthong ba'i phyir//de nyid rgyal mtshan phreng bar gsungs//bla re la sogs gang bres pa//dngos po la sogs dang po des//
3b7(rgyud): sems ni bla re zhes byar bshad//dam pa'i don las gsungs pa'o//gos phreng de dag thams cad ni//rnam pa sna tshogs snang ba nyid//gang zhig rdo rje 
4a1(rgyud): @##//rnam pa'i sems//dga' ba'i ye shes kyis ni mtshan//de yi 'og tu rtag tu gsungs//grwa yi mtshams ni thams cad du//rdo rje rin 
4a2(rgyud): chen rtogs pa'i phyir//rdo rje rin po che yis spras//zla ba nyi ma gang gsungs pa//thabs dang shes rab don mthong phyir//thabs dang shes rab bdag nyid sems 
4a3(rgyud): ye shes 'dis ni rnam par rgyan//sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje skyes//de nyid dkyil 'khor du ni gsungs//sku gsung thugs ni nye ba'i phyir//rnam pa gcig 
4a4(rgyud): tu mtshan nyid med//bla ma slob ma rnams gsal bar//rab tu 'jug pa de nyid gsungs//sbyin sreg yang ni bshad pa yang //lha rnams tshim pa nga yis 
4a5(rgyud): brjod||rnam rtog med pa'i sems gang ni//thab khung sgra yis mtshan ma nyid//bye brag phyed pas mi nang du//gzugs kyi phung sogs lnga po rnams//sems 
4a6(rgyud): las byung ba'i yam shing nyid//thams cad kyi ni bsreg lugs bya//gzugs la sogs pa gang byung ba//yam shing zhes ni rab tu grags//de ltar sbyin sreg 
4a7(rgyud): bshad nas ni//rab tu gnas pa brjod par bya//sku gsung thugs la rtag tu ni//gang zhig sems ni brtan gyur pa//de nyid rab tu gnas par brjod//
4b1(rgyud): phung po rnams kyi dengs po ci//gang zhig sngags kyi mtshan nyid ni//de ni gsal bar brjod par bya//yid rnams kyis ni skad du yang //gang zhig gsang ba bdag gis brjod//
4b2(rgyud): skye ba med cing snang med pa//mtshan gzhi mtshan nyid rnam par spangs//gsang ba'i don ni rtogs pa'i phyir//'di ni nga yis sngags su gsungs//sngags ni gsal bar bshad 
4b3(rgyud): nas ni//lha rnams yang ni brjod par bya//phung po khams dang skye mched kyi//rang bzhin gyi ni legs gnas gang //yang dag byang chub thar pa nyid//de kun yang dag rab 
4b4(rgyud): brtags shing //gal te sems kyis nyer brtags gang //lho zhes bya bar yang dag bshad//sku gsung thugs kyi phyag rgya las//de nyid du ni yang dag gsungs//lha la 
4b5(rgyud): gsol btab rnal 'byor ni//gsal ba rang yis brjod par bya//de nas skyil krung bcas nas ni//sna yi rtse mor bltas nas kyang //rang gi rang bzhin rnal 'byor 
4b6(rgyud): gyis||de nyid mthong bar bzhag par bya//snying ka'i gnas su lag pa gnyis//'bad pas mnyam par sbyar na su//mi rtog pa yi rang bzhin du//dge tshe sems ni 
4b7(rgyud): rab tu rgyu ba//sangs rgyas thams cad 'dus pa'i bdag/de tshe de la skye bar 'gyur//sangs rgyas thams cad bdus pa'i bdag/myur bar byin gyis rlob par 'gyur//
5a1(rgyud): @#//sangs rgyas thams cad 'dus pa'i bdag sangs rgyas kun gyi rtog pa'i bdag/thugs kyi rdo rjes gsungs pa bzhin//rnal 'byor sangs rgyas thams cad 'gyur//nga yis gsal bar 
5a2(rgyud): brjod pa ni//sku gsung thugs kyi grub sbyin pa//sangs rgyas kun mchog gsang ba'i don//thabs dang shes rab spyod yul don//sems can don du snying rje che//sangs rgyas byang chub rab sgrub pa//
5a3(rgyud): sna rtse dkyil 'khor nga yis gsungs//de dag thams cad dbyibs tsam du//mdor bsdus thams cad ma byas so//res 'ga' dam tshig 'khor lo yang //de nyid zhes bya ma gsungs so//rtogs 
5a4(rgyud): ba cung zad tsam gyis ni//gang 'grub de ni re 'ga' yang //gsung gi 'khor lor gsungs pa yin//rnam rtog med par lhag mos pas//sgrub po dam pas bsgrub par bya//drag dang khu bde bzhin 
5a5(rgyud): sha/bshang ba dang ni gci ba nyid//dam tshig zhes bya rab 'bad par//gang zhig bdag gis tshig gsungs pa//de rnams kun la de bzhin gshegs//de rnams tha snyad tsam gyis gnas//gang tshe 
5a6(rgyud): dam tshig 'dod chags sems//des ni de nyid rab tu spyod//gang tshe sems ni chags gyur pa//de ni sems can yod ma yin//de la kun tu rtog pa yi//khrag ni za bar 'gyur ba'o//khrag ni chen po 
5a7(rgyud): zos nas ni//'od dpag med pa'ang bza' bar bya//gang zhig chos rnams las skyes pa//khu bo'i skur ni bsgrags par bya//de tshe ma skyes rtogs pa'i phyir//bza' bar yang dag gsungs pa nyid//
5b1(rgyud): dngos po kun gyi mnyam pa nyid//sha chen du ni rab tu dbye//mi dmigs pa yi rnal 'byor gyis//bza' ba zhes byar mngon par brjod//gang zhig rab tu 'dzag pa'i sems//nus pa'i rjes su sbyor ba las//phyag rgya las kyi sbyor ba 
5b2(rgyud): yis//de yang gci ba'i sgra yis brjod//chos dang chos min nges pa'i sems//sbas pa'i char ni gnas pa 'di//chos rnams thams cad rab rtogs pa'o//bshad ba za bar yang dag brjod//dam tshig nyid kyi mtshan nyid ni//'di 
5b3(rgyud): rnams thams cad gsal bar bshad//'di rnams don ni rtogs pa yis//sangs rgyas kun gyi bdag nyid 'gyur//dpal bde ba dam pa rtogs par byed pa dang ldan pa zhes bya ba'i rgyud byin gyis brlab pa'i rim pa chen po 
5b4(rgyud): mkha' 'gro ma dra ba sdom pa'i brtag pa rnyog pa med pa zhes bya ba rdzogs so////rgya gar gyi mkhan po paN+Di ta ga ya d+ha ra dang /bod kyi lo ts+tsha ba dge slong shAkya ye shes kyis bsgyur ba'o////rgya gar skad du/shrI 
5b5(rgyud): bud+d+ha ka pA la nA ma yo gi nI tan+t+ra ra dze/bod skad du/dpal sangs rgyas thod pa zhes bya ba rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud kyi rgyal po//dpal sangs rgyas thod pa la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/
5b6(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang /gsung dang /thugs rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor chen po rgyan dang ldan pa'i dbus su/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad dang /rnal 'byor ma rnams dang lhan cig 
5b7(rgyud): tu bzhugs te/'phags pa kun dga' bo la sogs pa 'dod chags dang bral ba'i gtso bo rnams dang /'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug la sogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' bye ba phrag brgyad cu dang /
6a1(rgyud): @#//yang thabs cig tu bzhugs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis rdo rje'i tshig gis rdo rje chen po'i gnas su/rgyud thams cad dang sngags yang dag par bshad nas/de bzhin 
6a2(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyi bdag pos rdo rje dang padamo yang dag par sbyar bas/btsun mo'i b+ha gar yongs su mya ngan las 'das so//de yongs su mya ngan las 'da' pa mthong nas/byang chub 
6a3(rgyud): sems dpa' thams cad dang /rnal 'byor ma thams cad rmad du gyur te cig la cig lta zhing /e ma'o bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ba dag po dkyil 'khor chen po 
6a4(rgyud): rgyun dang ldan pa'i dbus su ji ltar yongs su mya ngan las 'a das/de nas rnal 'byor ma sna tshogs sde ma la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po phyag na rdo rjes 'di skad ces gsol to//lha 
6a5(rgyud): mo sems can bsod nams dman pa rnams nus pa la zhon par 'gyur ba'i thabs cung zad mchis sam//gsungs pa/rnal 'byor gyi rgyud la bye ba phrag drug nyid do//rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud la ni bye ba 
6a6(rgyud): phrag bcu drug nyid do//mdo sde so so la ni bye ba phrag brgyad cu'o//de bzhin du pha rol tu phyin pa'i theg pa ni bye ba la bsgres pa'i 'bum phrag lnga'o//'di dag thams cad ni thub pa'i dbang pos 
6a7(rgyud): gsungs so//sngags dang bral na ni rgyud kyis nus pa mi snang ste//rgyud la la ri ni ci nas sngags bye ba bzlas par gsungs so//bsod nams 'bring po rnams kyis ni sngags 'bum pas myur bar 
6b1(rgyud): 'grub par gsungs so//bsod nams che bas shin tu myur bar 'grub pa'o//de ltar rdo rje chen po rgyud kyi bdag po'i gtso bos gsungs so//bsod namasachung ba'i sems can rnams kyis ni 
6b2(rgyud): bye ba bzlas par gsungs so//sems can 'bad rtsol chung ba rnams kyis ni 'bum bzlas par bdag smra'o//sems can sgom pa la zhan pa rnams kyis ni khri kho na bzlas par bya'o//sems can 'dod 
6b3(rgyud): chags la zhugs pa rnams kyi bzlas pa ji ltar lags/blun po dang lkugs pa'i rigs can rnams kyi sngags kyi grangs ji ltar lags/shin tu dmigs pa'i sngags kyis bzlas pa ji ltar lags 
6b4(rgyud): 'di ltar phan chun smra bas bzlas na dngos grub gzhan du yang 'gyur ro//gal te dman pa'i sems kyis nyon mongs bzhin pas bzlas pa de'i tshe bskal pa skar ma lta bur yang de la dngos grub med 
6b5(rgyud): do||de ltar thos nas rnal 'byor ma sna tshogs sde ma bcom ldan 'das kyis zhal la lta bar byed cing zur mig dang bcas pa'i chags pas bltas te/shin tu khro bas 'jigs par byed cing snying 
6b6(rgyud): rje'i thugs la khrugs pas/bdud kyi dpung rab tu bcom mo//de ltar bzhugs pa'i lha mo chen mo rgyud thams cad kyi bdag po'i dbang phyug la de nas des gzigs pa tsam gyis bcom ldan 'das 
6b7(rgyud): kyis dbu las sngags phyung ngo //oM bud+d+hi sid+d+hi su sid+d+hi/a mri ta a ra dze bud+d+ha ka pA la s+phoT ne s+phoT ya t+sa sa ya ho hUM phaT//de ltar sngags kyi mchog son pas sa rim pa bdun gyi 'og tu chud 
7a1(rgyud): @#//pa'i klu rnams bcom par byas shing phye mar byas nas slar 'ongs te sna tshogs sde ma'i zhal du zhugs te/padama'i nang nas byung nas slar thod par zhugs so//sngags 'di'i mthus grub 
7a2(rgyud): rnams zhig ste/skrag cing rdul byung bar 'gyur ro//nor rgyas la sogs pa'i rdzu 'phrul chen po'i klu thams cad lhags te/stobs kyi rgyu dang /dung skyong dang /'jog po dang /mtha' yas dang /padam 
7a3(rgyud): chen po la sogs pa klu chen po rnams dang /gzhan gang yang rung ba dug las byung ba thams cad kyang lhags so//rnal 'byor ma sna tshogs sde ma la kha phyogs te klu thams cad kyis zhus pa/lha 
7a4(rgyud): mo bka' stsol ci zhig bgyi zhes smra'o//gsungs pa/gang la sangs rgyas nyams su myong ba de ltar gyis shig/de ltar thos pa tsam gyis dkyil 'khor chen po rgyan dang ldan pa'i dbus su 
7a5(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das kyis thod pa kha phye ba te glegs bam byung ngo//nam mkha' la sgra byung ngo//lha mo sna tshogs sde ma bzung //sems can kun la phan 'dogs ma//rgyud 'di rgyal po chen po ste//rnal 'byor 
7a6(rgyud): ma yi gong na med//sangs rgyas thod pa zhes bya ba//sems can kun la phan/byed pa'o//de ltar thos nas sna tshogs sde mas glegs bam yang dag par gzung ste//phyag na rdo rje la nye bar btang ngo//phyag 
7a7(rgyud): na rdo rjes rgyud 'di zung //sems can kun la' de 'bab pa//rnal 'byor ma yi dngos grub kun//'bras bu myur du ster byed pa'o//mnan dang spar ba ye shes che//gong na med pa rnyed dka' ba//gsang ba 
7b1(rgyud): mchog gi nyams dga' ba//lus kun la ni rnam par gnas//nam mkha' lding dang char dbab dang //dngul chu bcud kyis len pa dang //bskrad dang dbang du bya nyid dang //rmongs dang mngon spyod nyid dang ni//
7b2(rgyud): dpung la sogs pa rengs nyid dang //dus su zin pa bzlog pa dang //phur bus gdab dang bsad pa dang //rtag tu sgrub pa byed pa dang //sna tshogs sngags rims ji bzhin dang //ji bzhin rnal 'byor ma bskyed dang //
7b3(rgyud): dpal chen he ru ka yi ni//dus su zin pa bzlogs pa dang //bskyed pa dang ni gnas brgyad dang //dngos dang dngos med spros med dang //srid par spyod cing dgos med dang //dgongs pas bshad pa mchog chen dang //
7b4(rgyud): rnal 'byor ma yi mchog chen dang //rnal 'byor ma yi 'dus pa dang //nus pa rnam pa mang gsungs pa//thob pa tsam gyis 'grub par 'gyur//gzugs brnyan dag pa nyid dang ni//de bzhin bstan pa skye dang 
7b5(rgyud): dang //dur khrod 'jigs pa che bzang dang //ro langs sgrub pa la sogs dang //stobs po che rnams dgug pa dang //de bzhin 'jig rten gsum dgug dang //rims kyis gdung ba 'jig rten dang //dgra yi 
7b6(rgyud): kha sogs bcing ba dang //de bzhin bsad dang bslu ba dang //mi snang byed pa mchog dang ni//mig sman dang ni ril bu nyid//bcud len ral gri nyid dang ni//sangs rgyas thod pa'i sbyor ba yis//thos pa tsam 
7b7(rgyud): gyis 'grub par 'gyur//mi snang ba ni mchog chen dang //sna tshogs sgyu 'phrul rnam 'phrul dang //rang gi gzugs ni bsgyur ba dang //gzhan gyi lus la gzhug sogs dang //sangs rgyas phyogs rab bsrungs 
8a1(rgyud): @#//pa'i//sangs rgyas bu mo mchog chen rnams//khros pa tsam gyis 'gugs byed na//gzhan dang smos lta ci zhig dgos//mig 'phrul byed pa nyid dang ni//dge mtshan rnam pa mang shes 
8a2(rgyud): pa||'dod pa lnga ni bsten bzhin pas//'di ru 'grub par gdon mi za//'jigs shing 'joms pa'i rtsod chen po//gcig gis 'gum las rgyal bar byed//skyes pa gcig gis 'dod chen gyis//
8a3(rgyud): bud med 'bum phrag 'jig byed cing //rdo rjer byed pa rnyed dga' ba//gcig gis thams cad 'dod pa spyad//de nas gzhan yang rab bshad pa//mkha' lding sbyor ba rim ji bzhin//rgyud kun du yang rnyed dka' 
8a4(rgyud): ba||mu stegs rnams kyi yul min pa//rgyud kun du yang ma gsungs so//sangs rgyas thod pa'i gzhung ji bzhin//rdo rje mgon pos gsungs pa yi//mkha' lding shin tu rmad byung ba//'jig rten gsum 
8a5(rgyud): na rnyed dka' ba'i//sbyor ba rnam pa bco brgyad gsungs//sha li ka yi sbyor ba dang //rig pa 'dzin dang rgyan po 'gyed//rnga rngu bud med 'phrog pa dang //shin bsnun zhags pas 'ching ba dang //khu byug 
8a6(rgyud): padams 'byed pa dang //stags byed chu blugs dung 'bud dang //thod lo kor 'phrog skyug pa dang //ras g.yog thal lcags brdab pa dang //yig gsum bsam gtan gyis ni gtam//'di rnams kyis ni 
8a7(rgyud): dgug par bya//mkha' lding sbyor ba bco brgyad po//'jig rten gsum na rab rnyed dka'//sems can rnams la phan byed pa//rdo rje mgon po nyid kyis gsungs//de nas gzhan yang rab bshad pa//mchog 
8b1(rgyud): gi ye shes ji bzhin pa//b+ha ga'i dbus su rnam par bzhugs//bde ba chen po ster byed pa//'di nyid du ni kun gnas kyang //byis pa rmongs pas mi shes so//der ni bcud dang bcud len te//de nyid du ni sangs rgyas bzhugs 
8b2(rgyud): der ni dkyil 'khor bzang po'o//der ni slob ma dbang bskur ba'o//der ni he ru ka dang 'o//nyi shu rtsa bzhi'i dbang gtso bo//der ni nam mkha' lding 'jigs pa//de nyid du ni ro langs sgrub//der ni dur khrod brgyad po 
8b3(rgyud): rnams//skad cig tsam gyis bskyed pa'i rgyu//der ni sangs rgyas bdag nyid te//stong phrag gsum ni rnam par bzhugs//der ni 'jig rten khams kun te//de nyid bcom ldan 'das lha mo//sangs rgyas la 
8b4(rgyud): sogs bskyed pa'i yum//byang chub sems dpa' dpal chen po//shes rab pha rol phyin dpung gnyen//de nyid bsod nams kun ster ma//de nyid thar pa ster byed ma//phyag rgya chen po grags chen po//dngos 
8b5(rgyud): grub kun ni 'dod byed ma//ma dang tshangs mo sring mo dang //bu mo bud med rab brtan ma//shes rab mnyes shing 'jigs byed ma//'gro drug bde sdug bag chags bcas//'khor ba mya ngan 'das nyid dang //
8b6(rgyud): bdag nyid chen po de nyid de//rnam rtog kun tu rtog pa nyid//de bzhin 'char ba nub pa dang //nyin mtshan ma la stan bza' ba dang //nges pa'i brtul zhugs rtsis dang grangs//rnam par mi rtog yang 
8b7(rgyud): dag rtog/dman dang mchog dang bar ma dang //gzhan dang gang dang de dang ni//de nyid bdag nyid chen po gcig/sems can kun la bde ba 'bab//de ltar thos nas phyag na rdo rje la sogs pa'i byang chub 
9a1(rgyud): @#//sems dpa' rnams dang //'dod can ma la sogs pa ri rab kyi rdul phra rab snyed kyi rnal 'byor ma rnams dga' ba'i spyan gdangs nas sna tshogs sde ma la yang dbang rang du btung nas//'di ltar 
9a2(rgyud): gsol to//e ma'o//sna tshogs sde ma rnal 'byor ma//sems can kun la bde 'ba' ma//glegs bam don dang ldan gzhig/de kun sangs rgyas byin rlabs bshad//de nas phyag na rdo rje'i phyag gnyis 
9a3(rgyud): glang po che'i lag pa ltar rab tu brkyang ste/glegs bam yang dag par blangs so//de nas sangs rgyas kyi byin rlabs kyis phyag rgya chen po'i rdo rje'i gnas su don dang bcas pa'i gzhung zhu bar gyur to//phyag na rdo 
9a4(rgyud): rje'i snying ga nas byung ste//sna tshogs sde ma'i thugs su bal so so//de nas phyag na rdo rje gnod sbyin gyi bdag po chen pos rnal 'byor ma chen mo la 'di skad gsol to//sngags kyi bzlas pa gang zhig lags//
9a5(rgyud): las kyi tshogs rnams ji ltar 'grub//sngon du snyen pa gang zhig lags//mkha' lding 'grub pa'i nges pa gang //dngul chu bcud len sna tshogs pa//dngos grub ster byed gang zhig lags//sbar dang 
9a6(rgyud): mnan pa gang gsungs dang //dus dang bya ba'i nges bcas dang /dngos grub thob dus gang zhig lags//sangs rgyas ji lta bur brjod//de bzhin thod pa ji lta bu//sbyor ba dang ldan pa'i ji lta bu//sna tshogs 
9a7(rgyud): sde ma'ang ji lta bu//theg pa chen po ji lta bu//rnal 'byor ma yi rnam nges ci//gsungs pa/rgyud 'dir sngon du bsnyen pa med//de bzhin yul dang dus chos dang //gza' dang rgyu skar sbyor med pa//blo 
9b1(rgyud): dang ldan pas rtag tu brten//dus dang bya ba'i nges mi bya//sangs rgyas thod pa'i sbyor ba yis//thos pa tsam gyis 'grub par 'gyur//sangs rgyas rdo rje zhes su gsungs//thod pa gru gsum yang dag 'byung //sbyor ba 
9b2(rgyud): gsang bar gnas pa'i rtsa//de yi sna tshogs ngo mtshar byed//sna ni bde chen ye shes so//'di nyid theg pa chen po ste//rnal 'byor ma yi rtsa las byung //de ni rmad byung che mchog ste//sangs rgyas thod pa'i sbyar ba 
9b3(rgyud): nyid//de nyid he ru ka dpa'o//'khor ba'i 'jigs las sgrol byed pa//sangs rgyas thod pa'i sbyong ba yis//sangs rgyas kun gyi bde ba 'bab//rnal 'byor chen mo sna tshogs bde//dpa' dang bdag mo dbang 
9b4(rgyud): phyug ma//de yi thod pa'i dkyil 'khor la//sngags dang rgyud ni rnam par gnas//de yi ye shes bzang po ni//lhan cig skyes pa dag las 'byung //de ni rigs pa'i dbang lnga dang //sangs rgyas bdun gyi 
9b5(rgyud): mtshan can te//sna tshogs sbyor dang tshe 'phel dang //dngos grub thams cad sgrub pa ni//sangs rgyas thod pa'i spyor ba yis//'grub par 'dir ni gdon mi za//man ngag lam las gang shes pa//bla ma'i 
9b6(rgyud): drin las shes par bya//sangs rgyas thod pa'i rgyud chen po rnal 'byor ma rnams kyi gdon med pa gsang ba'i yang shin tu gsang ba zhes bya ba las dngos grub thams cad dang ye shes gtan la dbab pa dang //
9b7(rgyud): thod pa kha phye ba las glegs bam byung ba'i le'u ste dang po'o//de nas yang dag rab bshad pa//sngags le'u ji bzhin gsungs pa ste//mkha' 'gro ma mo 'byung po rnams//sngags gang gis ni 'grub 
10a1(rgyud): @#//'gyur ba//de ni bdag gis rab bshad kyis//khyod kyis dad pa brtan pas nyon//oM bud+d+ha ka pA li ni taT s+phoT ya sarba shu b+he pra dA li ni hUM phaT//spos dbul ba'i sngags so//oM bud+d+ha ka pAli ni 
10a2(rgyud): ma Ta ma Ta a swAhA//me tog dbul ba'i sngags so//au ma hA ka pA la badzri ba ra ba taT taT staT ya staT ya maT maT hUM he hrIHswAhA//dri dbul ba'i sngags so//A+oM bud+d+ha ka pA li nai gr-ih+Na gr-ih+Na gr-ih+Na pa ya gr-ih+Na 
10a3(rgyud): pa ya ba ra ba ra ta Ta ta Ta/staT ya staT ya maT hUM hUM hUM swAhA//mang me dbul ba'i sngags so//oM bud+d+ha ka pA lini/gr-ih+Na gr-ih+Na gr-ih+Na ba khA hUM/lha bshos dbul ba'i sngags so//oM ma hA ka pA li ni badzra d+harma d+harma/
10a4(rgyud): me b+he ba re pra ba re ba ra ba dkar kar taT ta Ta ha he swAhA//gos dbul ba'i sngags so/oM hUM heM hoM hiM hiM ha/go cha'i sngags so//oM bud+ng+ha ka pA li ni a khaM kro d+huM shrI aHA hUM hUM hUM phaT//spyan drangs pa'i 
10a5(rgyud): sngags so//oM ma hA ka pA libadzri NaM gats+tsha gats+tsha ud tiShaTaHa/dza dza dza ha dzi dzaM dzaM/tai lo kyaM biT pa Ni hUM phaT//gshegs su gsol ba'i sngags so//oM mahA ka pA la badzra tsi tre su tsi tre a laM ba ni phran yA/sarba 
10a6(rgyud): b+hu ta na ma ra Na kI la ya sarba duSh+TAn uts+tshuT ya sarba bi g+hAM bi dra ba ya b+ha ga ba ti yak+Sha rAk+Sha sa be shA tsa na sho ba ya ta Ta ya sap+ta pA tA la ga ta na/sa ra sa ra/pra sa ra pra sa ra/hUM hUM hUM hUM phuT phaT phaT//
10a7(rgyud): 'byung po thams cad pa'i ga tor ma'i sngags so//'bras sa lu bAngs te 'o mas bkrus la bu mo gzhon nu mas de shin tu zhib par brtag par bya/de yang shes rab can gyis gzugs 
10b1(rgyud): brnyan byas te//zhal cig phyag bzhi pa bzhung bzhin/cang te'u dang /kha T+waM ga dang /ral gri dang /thod pa 'dzin pa'o//de nas ro bsregs pa'i sol bas phye mar byas la rdo rje gtsug gis mnye bar bya'o//de nas 
10b2(rgyud): des gzugs brnyan la byug par bya'o//me tog dkar pos mchod pa ri ra byas te//sangs rgyas thod pa'i rnal 'byor bas he ru ka'i rnam pa'i sbyor bas sngags brgya rtsa brgyad bzlas par ba'o//oM ma hA 
10b3(rgyud): badzra he ru ka badzre/d+ha ra d+hi ra/d+hu ru d+hu ru/mahA dz+nyA na swA+o Ta ne shI g+h+raM sA d+ha ya staM b+ha yA kI la ya hUM phaT//sngags 'di chu la lan bdun du mngon par bzlas te/gzugs brnyan de bkru bar bya'o//chu des rang 
10b4(rgyud): gi gdong bkrus na/mi snang bar byed de/lha rnams kyis kyang rnyed par dka'o//nags kyi nang du stag gis kyang mi mthong ngo//gzhan dag gis kyang de mthong bar mi 'gyur ro//srog chags 'bum gyi nang 
10b5(rgyud): du sang na ngo mtshar sna tshogs byed do//gang gis kyang mi mthong ngo/lha'i bu mo 'phrog cing lha rnams kyis kyang mi mthong ngo//'o mas gdong bkrus pa tsam gyis mi mthong bar 'gyur ro//gzugs brnyan de'i 
10b6(rgyud): snying kar shang 'ba' bo las phul te/dur khrod kyi me la 'ba' bo las mtshangs pa rdo rjes rab tu sbar la/de'i thal ba yang dag par blangs te/kham phor du bzhag par bya'o//yang na padama'i snod du'o//sngags 
10b7(rgyud): 'di brgya rtsa brgyad du mngon par bzlas par bya'o//oM bud+d+ha ka pA li ni taM hUM/oM badzra li si nI hrIHhUM/oM ka mi nI khaM hUM/oM pA tA lA ba sI mA AHhUM/se na oM tsa tra/se nA sa ma ya tiSh+Tha ya hrIHhUM/sngags 'di 
11a1(rgyud): @#//dag ni sangs rgyas rnams kyis kyang shin tu rnyed par dka'o//thal ba de dang rdo rje btsug gis phyag par byas nas/ji srid du lus la mtshon chas 'og pa de srid du mi tshugs so//slar zhing 
11a2(rgyud): dgra thams cad kyi mgo bo 'chad par 'gyur ro//gang sngar ba la bltas na de long bar 'gyur ro//gang zhig sngar ba'i sems 'dzin pa smyo bar 'gyur ro//thal ba de nyid dang mar khu bzang pos mig byugs na 
11a3(rgyud): dpag tshad stong mthong bar 'gyur ro//ga'i ci yang rung bas 'og na gnas pa'i gter dpag tshad stong gi 'og tu son pa'i dngul dang gser dang rin po che gang na yod pa de thams cad la srin lag gis 
11a4(rgyud): bsdigs mdzub byas pa tsam gyis thams cad mdun du lhags te gsol ba 'debs so//kyai sangs rgyas thod pa'i rnal 'byor pa gter long la sems can gyi don gyis shig/der gter blangs na gang 'dod 
11a5(rgyud): pa de byed do//thal ba de dang go ro tsa na lhan cig bsnos te/mdzub mos mnyes pas dpung gi tshogs langs pa mdzub mo bskor ba tsam gyis 'bum phrag stong gi dpung rnams khas phub tu ltung bar 'gyur ro//
11a6(rgyud): gang 'dod pa de byed do//bkrus pa tsam gyis langs par 'di la the tsom med do//thal ba de dang na nye'i phye ma dang bsnos te cig tu byas la/rdo rje'i gtsug rjes su byin te khab len gyi lcags kyi phye ma 
11a7(rgyud): bsre zhing gser gyi phye ma yang cung zad bsre bar bya'o//rdo rje'i chu'i khu ba dang lhan cig tu bsnos te za ma na mi sngon du nyi ma bdun gyi bar du bzhag go/de nas 'khris pa chen po mnyes te rta'i khrag dang 
11b1(rgyud): lhan cig tu bsnon la yang snod de nyid du nyi ma gsum bzhag par bya'o//de nas gton te lcags kyi snod du bzhag par bya'o//zangs ma'i snod du bzhag pa de me'i nang du bcug na gser dang dngul du 'gyur bar som 
11b2(rgyud): nyi ma da do//srad gcig kyang srang stong gi rin thang du 'gyur te rin po che dang 'dra bar 'gyur ro//gser de ma sha cig dang dngul srang cig bsres te me la bzhu na srang sum brgya'i thang gi tshad du 'gyur bar 'di 
11b3(rgyud): la the tsom med do//'di lta bu'i sbyor ba ni sangs rgyas rnams kyis kyang rnyed par dka'o//'jig rten dbang phyug la sogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' grags pa chen po snying rje dang ldan pa'i bdag nyid chen po 
11b4(rgyud): stobs che ba de rnams kyis kyang sbyin par mi nus so//'di yi sbyor ba rnyed dka' ba//sangs rgyas thod pa'i rgyud du gsung //rgyud gzhan rnams su sbas pa yin//'dus sogs rnal 'byor rgyud du ni//sbyor ba 
11b5(rgyud): rnam pa ma gsungs pa//gang tshe sngon du bsnyen de'i tshe//de yi sbyor bas ci zhig bya//bsod nams dman pa'i sems can gang //mtshams med lnga ni byed pa yang //sangs rgyas thod pa'i sbyor ba yin//'di lta'i 
11b6(rgyud): sbyor ba 'grub par 'gyur//dngos po de yis blo chen rnams//sems can don la gcig tu brtson//srang cig gis bsres bye ba'i rin//sbyor ba kun tu rnyed par dka'//sbyor ba glegs bam la mi bri//
11b7(rgyud): bla ma'i drin las shes par bya//bla mas dngos grub bla mas thar//bla ma ston pa rnams kun pa//des na slob ma blo can gyis//'bad pas bla ma mnyes dar bya//thal ba de nyid kyis ltas lta ba'i don du bu mo'i 
12a1(rgyud): @#//mgo la rmugs te rdo rje gtsug gis bu mo bkru'o//yang thal ba'i thig le byas la/tshangs pa rdo rjes maN+Dal gru bzhi byas te/de'i dbus su bu mo bzhag par bya'o//bdug spos lngas 
12a2(rgyud): bdugs shing sangs rgyas thams cad kyis byin gyis brlabs na bu mo la 'bab par 'gyur te/'das pa dang /ma 'ongs pa dang da ltar byung ba ston to//bu don gyis 'bras la sngags 'di lan bdun du mngon 
12a3(rgyud): par sngags so//oM khu d+d+ha ka pA li ni de he/de hi da dA pa ya da dA pa ya he he b+ha ga pa ti shI swA/bad gaM d+ha ra hi bud+d+ha ka pA la badzra s+ya ya dz+ha ma laM g+ha ya hUM staM hri phaT a phaT/yungs kar snun pa tsam gyis ni//
12a4(rgyud): bu mo grags chen ma ni dgod//de yi kha nas ral gri bdun//'byung ba la ni the tshom med//sangs rgyas thod pa'i rnal 'byor skad cig ma de nyid la 'grub par 'dir som nyid med do//de ltar ral gcis 
12a5(rgyud): grub pas bye ba brgya stong gi rig 'dzin 'grub par bskor bar 'gyur ro//'di lta bu'i sbyor ba ni 'jig rten gsum na yang rnyed par dka'o//bu mo bkrus pa tsam gyis rnal 'byor mar 'gyur ro//de'i 
12a6(rgyud): thal ba lcags gsum gyis bkris te ril bu byas nas khar bcug pa tsam gyis ni mi snang bar 'gyur ro//shes rab can gyis sum cu pa yang bskor ro//sangs rgyas thams cad kyis kyang mi gzig 
12a7(rgyud): so//ril bu lag par blangs pas dngos po gang dang gang la reg pa tsam gyis de dbang du 'gyur ro//thal ba'i sbyor ba ri 'di dag ni//sangs rgyas kyis kyang rnyed par dka'//oM bud+d+ha ka pA la dza hUM kha 
12b1(rgyud): ra aM 'jig rten gsum sgrub pa'i sngags so//oM bud+d+ha ke pA li ni hrIHhUM/dgra brlag pa'i sngags so//oM ma hA ka pA la badzrI kha pa khA hi khA hi sarba duSh+TA na g+ha g+ha g+ha ta ya g+hA ta ya sarba sha truM aM ha tri hUM phaT/bsnyad 
12b2(rgyud): pa'i sngags so//sangs rgyas thod pa'i rgyud chen po//rnal 'byor ma rnams kyi gong na med pa gsang ba'i yang shin tu gsang bzhis bya ba las thal ba'i sbyor ba dang las sna tshogs gtan la dbab pa dang /sngags kyi 
12b3(rgyud): le'u ste gnyis pa'o////de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po phyag na rdo rjes sna tshogs sde ma la btud de/'di skad ces gsol to//sems can de na la gcig brtson pa//
12b4(rgyud): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das la mo//'di lta'i rab tu sbyor zhes pa//rgyud gzhan dag tu ma gsungs shing //sangs rgyas kun gyis rab spas pa//de ni 'bad de gsal bar mdzod//lha mo chen mo khyod 
12b5(rgyud): kyis gsungs//lha yi le'u ni ji lta bu//sangs rgyas thod pa rnal 'byor ma'i//rgyud du lha rnams du zhig gsungs//de ru gzugs ni ji lta bu//gus pa yis ni nyan par 'tshal//gsungs pa/raM las nyi ma 
12b6(rgyud): bsam par bya//nyi ma'i steng du hUM byung ba'i//rdo rje nag po sreg pa'i bdag/rdo rje de nyid 'od zer gyis//rab dra ba'i mtshams bcing ngo//de bzhin rdo rje na 'gyur ba//rdo rje sa bzhi bsam par bya//rdo rje'i sa gzhi'i dbus 
12b7(rgyud): chung bar//e yi rnam pa bdag nyid che//gru gsum khams gsum bdag nyid can//yi ge lam gyur sa yi ni//dkyil 'khor gru bzhi rab ser po'o//de yi gsungs nas sa bon ni//yi ge oM ste chos bdag nyid//
13a1(rgyud): @#//oM las byung ba'i 'khor lo ni//rtsa rnams kun gyi rang bzhin can//rnal 'byor ma ni rnam par bzhugs//rtsa ni drug cu la rgyan dang //sprul pa drug cu rtsa bzhi 'dab//des na rtsa brgya nyi shu'o//rdo rje sku 
13a2(rgyud): yis bdag nyid che//shes rab can gyis de ltar bsam//de yi dbus gnas sa bon ni//be dkar yi ge las byung ba//chu yi dkyil 'khor zlum po'o//rtsa rnams thams cad chu yi ni//dkyil 'khor la ni thim par 
13a3(rgyud): 'gyur/slar yang bsam par bya ba ni//rab dmar las byung me'i dkyil 'khor//gru gsum 'bar ba'i bdag nyid can//rab tu 'bar bzhin pa yi ni//rtsa ba'i 'khor lo yang dag blta//de nas bsam par bya ba 
13a4(rgyud): ni//yaM gyur rlung gi dkyil 'khor ni//zla ba byed pa'i rnam 'dra sngo//rtsa rnams rab tu g.yo bar gzugs//thim gyur 'khor lo'i rig par gsungs//de dag thams cad thim gyur pa//de yi dbus gnas rnal 
13a5(rgyud): 'byor pa//de ltar shes par byed gang zhig/sangs rgyas thod pa'i rnal 'byor pas//de nas zla ba bsam par bya//zla ba'i steng du nyi ma'o//de yi dbus chud sa bon ni//hUM dang a yi rang bzhin no//yi ge ka yis 
13a6(rgyud): ha rgyan ste/ka ni ha la thim par 'gyur//mi phyed pa yi ha dang hUM//gnyi ga'i bdag nyid can blta'o//ha ni mi shigs shes rab kyi//bdag nyid u dang yi ge dag/gnyi ga gcig tu gyur pa ni//yi ge hUM gi mtsho 
13a7(rgyud): lta'o//hUM las byung ba'i 'od zer rnams//de yis bde ba che bskul te//ba mo lta bu'i ri bong can//nyi mar lhan cig zhu gyur cing //dkyil 'khor gnyi ga gcig gyur pa//nyi ma'i mchog tu blta bar bya//
13b1(rgyud): yang ni hUM byung 'od zer des//rtsa ba'i 'khor lo thams cad la//reg ma thag pa'i mod la ni//rtsa yi 'khor lo las 'bab 'gyur//zhu ba'i de yis sa bon bkru//hUM gyur pa las rdo rje ste//dbus su de nyid kyis byin 
13b2(rgyud): rlabs//sa bon rdo rje las byung ba//dpa' bo khrag 'thung mdog sngon pa//zhal gcig pa la phyag bzhi pa//gtso bo hUM zhes 'don pa ste//skyil krung phyed pa'i gar gyis gnas//gnyi ga'ang khro ba'i bdag nyid can//
13b3(rgyud): stag gi lpags pa'i na gza' banabs//mgo bo rlon pa'i phreng ba 'chang //bse gsum mchod phyir thogs pa yi//shin tu gzhad pas 'jigs che ba'i//gtso bo snying rje'i bdag nyid blta//dpung rgyan mgul 
13b4(rgyud): do 'khor gdub dang //gcer bu rus pa'i ska rags 'ching //g.yer ka bcas pa'i rkang gdub can//ma lus bdud rnams 'joms par byed//ral pa'i gcod pan yang dag ldan//sangs rgyas rnam pa lnga yis 
13b5(rgyud): rgyan//zla ba phyed pa 'dzin pa'i lha//dbu la sna tshogs rdo rjes mnan//'khor lo rna cha gad bu dang //phyag rgya rnam pa lnga yis mtshan//thal ba'i rdul can 'jigs par byed//thod pa gti mug gis rgyan pa'i 
13b6(rgyud): dpa' bo 'khor ba sgrol byed pa//byang chub sa bon rang bzhin no//gtso chen de ye shes rab ma//sna tshogs sde ma rnal 'byor ma//gcer bu mgo bo'i phreng ba spang //rgyan med mchod phyir thogs med ma//
13b7(rgyud): bde ba kun gyi gzugs can ma//phyag gnyis sgra ni grol ba'o//he ru ka las 'khyud gnas ma//de ltar pha rol phyin lnga mo//kha dog dmar mo rnams gsal ma//zhal ni shin tu rnam 'gyur ba//
14a1(rgyud): @#//cung zad mche ba gtsigs 'jigs ma//g.yas na cang te'u gri gug ste//g.yon na kha TaM thod pa'o//rang gi lus skyes 'od zer gyis/rtsa yi 'khor lo bskul bar bya//de yis bskul 
14a2(rgyud): ba tsam gyis ni//rtsa ba 'khor lo rang bzhin las//'dzag 'gyur 'dor ni the tsom med//'dzag pa de las rnal 'byor ma//brgyad po bu mo rnams kyi mchog/mchod pa sna tshogs 'dzin pa yis//mgon 
14a3(rgyud): po he ru ka mchod do//rdo rje mgon po'i shar gnas pa//rdo rje mchog pa ma yis ni//thod pa khrag gis bkang bzung ste//gzi brjid chen po gsol bar mdzad//rdo rje pho nya bdag nyid che//dbang ldan du ni 
14a4(rgyud): cang te'u rdung //rdo rje gdong ma dri sdod dang //me yis phyogs su rnam par gnas//rdo rje can ma grags chen mo//mched yon snod can bden bral na//rdo rje gtsigs ma lho yi ni//phyag na rnga bo che 'dzin 
14a5(rgyud): gsungs//rlung du rdo rje lte ba ma//ri bong can gyi snod 'dzin no//nub tu rdo rje 'jigs ma ni//lce spyang phyag na 'dzin par gsungs//byang du 'dod chags rdo rje ma//lha mo'i chags pas gtso bo mchod//
14a6(rgyud): de ltar he ru ka bsam nas//rnal 'byor ma ni brgyad kyis mchod//sna tshogs zas dang sna tshogs skam//sna tshogs rol mo mchog gsungs dang //dri zhim lnga dang me tog spos//'di dag gis ni gtso bo 
14a7(rgyud): mchod//mchod yon zhal bsil de bzhin khrus//gru yi mchog ni rnam mang pos//mgon mo khrag 'thung bdag nyid che//'di dag gis ni mchod par bya//dpa' bo rdzogs par mthong nas ni//rtsa yi 'khor 
14b1(rgyud): lo las ni 'dzag/'dzag pa de las rnal 'byor ma//rnam par brtse ba cher ldan pa//bde gshegs kun gyis byin rlabs ma//sna tshogs rgyan gyis rnam par klubs//sna tshogs pa yin bza' brdab so//ma thugs 
14b2(rgyud): ni lang tsho gzhon pas rgyan//lo ni bcu dang gnyis lo par//phyag g.yas byang chub sems bkang ba'i//bum pa a ma'i lUg bcas bsnams//zhal ni rab bzhad dang ba yis//he ru ka ni rgyal gyur cig/
14b3(rgyud): rgyal gyur cig ces 'di ltar smra//mgon pos mdun du bltas nas ni//dbang bskur mchog ste gsol ba gdab//rtse dang ldan pa'i grags chen mo//'khor ba'i lam du bying ba bdag/dbang bskur ba yis 
14b4(rgyud): bdag sgral phyir//rdo rje rnal 'byor mas khrus shig/dper na bu tsha med pa'i khyim//shi ba tsam gyis stong ba nyid//de bzhin dbang dang bral na ni//ye shes kun gyi stongs pa nyid//dbang na pi bang sbyor kun 
14b5(rgyud): tshogs//rgyud dang bral na rdung mi rung //de bzhin dbang dang bras na ni//sngags dang bsam gtan 'grub mi 'gyur//rmongs pa bang zhig dbang med par//bdag ni dbang bskur zhes smra ba//ji srid sangs rgyas 
14b6(rgyud): bzhugs kyi bar//slob mar bcas te dmyal bar 'gro//de ltar shes bya rnal 'byor pas//slar yang bdag la dbang bskur cig/yang dang yang du phyag 'tshal te//thal mo sbyar nas gsol ba gdab//
14b7(rgyud): gal te dbang bskur med na ni//he ru ka ru bdag gyur cig/de nas rnal 'byor ma de rnams//rgyal gyur rgyal gyur zhes smra zhing //bcom ldan dbang bskur khrus gsol lo//phyi nang gsang bar thams 
15a1(rgyud): @#//pa yi//dbang bskur tsam gyis nam mkha' yi//dkyil nas rnga yi sgra grags cing //me tog tsam dan char 'bab 'gyur//de nas rnal 'byor ma de rnams//rtsa ba'i 'khor lo la ni 
15a2(rgyud): thim//sangs rgyas thod pa'i rgyud rnal 'byor ma'i gong na med pa gsang ba'i yang shin tu gsang ba zhes bya ba las/lha bskyed pa dang /dbang bskur ba'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////
15a3(rgyud): rdo rje sde la phyag byas nas//'di skad ces gsol to//ci yi phyir na bdag cag rnams//'od zer gyis ni 'tshal te mchis//bcom ldan bdag la bshad du gsol//de nas phyag na rdo rje gnod sbyin gyi 
15a4(rgyud): bdag pos rnal 'byor ma sna tshogs sde ma la 'di skad ces gsol to/nor rgyas la sogs bdag nyid che//klu rnams ci'i phyir dkrugs pa lags//rnal 'byor chen po bdag la gsungs//de nas sna tshogs 
15a5(rgyud): sde ma'i snying gi 'od zer gyis klu thams cad kyi snying ma dang spyi bo'i mthar thug par zhugs te/tshim par byas nas slar log go/de nas klu rnams thams cad kyis phyag 'tshal te//'di skad ces gsol te/
15a6(rgyud): lha mo bka' ni ci zhig gsol//kye lags ji lta bu zhig bgyi//gsungs pa/mu stegs la sogs mkha' lding sngags//ye shes lam byung ji lta bu//dus su zin pa ji ltar yin//ji ltar dus ni skye 
15a7(rgyud): bar 'gyur//sbrul byi ba zin pa tsam gyis ni//slar yang sems can 'cha' gyur zhing //zhus pa/khyab 'jug dbang phyug sogs lha dang //tshangs pa tshangs pa bdun na 'don//de rnams la ni 'chi 'pho 
15b1(rgyud): 'chis//dug gi nyes pas gnod par 'gyur//de ltar dus su zin ces smras//sbrul gyi dug ni sel ba med//de bzhin sbrul la dug ni yod//sbrul zin 'tsho 'am mi 'tsho ba//bde byed la sogs rdzu 'phrul 
15b2(rgyud): cha'i//lha de rnams ni de ltar smra//stong pa stong nyid des gsungs pa//bcom ldan rdo rje 'chang bas gsungs//chos rnams thams cad stong pa la//sbrul gyi dug ni ji ltar spyod//gang gis 'dzin cing gang 
15b3(rgyud): zhig 'dzin//dus dang dus min ji ltar 'gyur//ji ltar spyod cing ji lta bsdu//gang phyir 'dzin cing gang du 'dzin//sems can dug zin mi 'chi ma//stong nyid smra ba'i nges pa yin//'dod chags gti mug stong 
15b4(rgyud): pa nyid//grags chen yul ni stong pa nyid//yul gyi rang bzhin dri ma med//dri ma dang bral ji ltar dug/sbrul gyis kyang ni ci zhig byed//tha mal ba yi yul ni sbrul//yul gyi nyes pa dug nyid do//lha rnams 
15b5(rgyud): yul rnams kyis ni bsgribs//thams cad dus su zin pa yin//yul gyi nyes pa sbyangs pa nyid//stong pa longs spyod gos mi 'gyur//sangs rgyas thod pa'i rnal 'byor pas//'dod kun nye bar longs spyod la//
15b6(rgyud): re skegs sbyor ba rnyed dka' dang //sangs rgyas thod pa'i sbyor ldan pas//'dir ni klu rnams thams cad lhags//de phyir rab nges lha mo kye//dus zin the tshom med par//blo med mu stegs can de smra//'di ltar smras 
15b7(rgyud): te klu rnams kyis//sna tshogs sde la phyag 'tshal te//rang rang gi ni gnas su dong //de nas bcom ldan 'das mas yul gyi nyes pa spong ba'i sngags gsungs pa//oM shU n+ya tA dz+nyA na badzra swa b+hA wa At+ma ko hi//
16a1(rgyud): @#//dang stong min mtha' dag bcas//sgyu ma'i rmi lam lta bur cig/'jig rten mtha' dag brdzun gyis bcings//kye lags bshes mo 'di ltar ltos//da nas phug na rdo rje gnod sbyin gyi 
16a2(rgyud): bdag po chen pos bcom ldan 'das ma'i zhabs la spyi bo sa gtugs te/'di skad ces gsol te//ri skegs sbyor ba rnyed dka' ba//lha mo yi ni bshad du gsol//sems can don la gcig tu 'bad//
16a3(rgyud): de ru bdag la ltad mo mchis//gsungs pa/dkyil 'khor med par sbyin mi bya//slob ma bsdu bar mi bya'o//gtso bos ji ltar gsungs pa yi//dkyil 'khor las ni smra bar bya//mtho lnga pa yis bzung 
16a4(rgyud): ste//khor yug du ni gru bzhi'o//de yi phyed kyi zlum po la//brgyad las 'dam skyes 'dab ma brgyad//oM hUM hriHhUM khaM hUM a hUM tiShaTaHa tiShaTaHa ya a d+hiShaTaHa yed//sa sbyar ba'i sngags so//badzra he ru 
16a5(rgyud): ka badzra sa ma ya hi sarba duSh+Ta sarba ma ya mu drA pA b+haM dza ha hUM ru lu ru lu b+h+yo hUM hUM phaT//sa sbyang ba'i dus su gtor ma btang ba'i sngags so//oM sarba ta thA ga ta oM d+hiShaTaHa na i dam sU tra tiShaTaHa ma tsa la he he bud+d+ha 
16a6(rgyud): ka pA la badzra he ru ka hUM hUM tha tha//sngags 'ongs lan bdun du srad bu la mdzad par sngags par bya'o//thig skud kyi sngags so//gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa//rta babs bzhi dang yang dag ldan//kha khyer 
16a7(rgyud): snam bu spangs pa'o//ba dan rnam pa brgyad kyis rgyan//sangs rgyas thod pa'i sbyor ba yis//rdul tshon gzhan dang 'dres med pa'i//rdul mtshon dmar por rdzogs par bya//da ru 
16b1(rgyud): bum pa ba'i po rnams//kA dang gra ru rnam par bzhag/bum pa mtshan nyid bri bya ba//thod pa dang ni kha T+waM ga/gri gug dang ni cang te'u dang //cang te'u gnas su rdo rje bri//rdo rje'i steng du 'das skyes te//
16b2(rgyud): de ltar bum par bis byas nas//chu yis yongs su dgang bar bya//bum pa la na ras kyis rgyan//rin chen rnam pa lnga gzhug cing //buM par kun nas 'bru lnga dang //a mra'i lcug mas dgang por 
16b3(rgyud): bya//de nas 'o ma'i snod bzhag te//rdo rje slob dpon blo chen gyis//sangs rgyas thod pa'i sbyor dang ldan//pad+ma'i steng du nyi ma nas//nyi ma'i steng du hUM yig bsam//hUM gyur pa las rdo rje na//sngon po hUM 
16b4(rgyud): gi bdag nyid skyes//hUM dang rdo rje byung dpa' bo//he ru ka ni bdag nyid che//sku mdog sngon po phyag bzhi pa//gtso bo chen po zhal gcig pa//g.yas na gri gug thod pa ste//g.yon na kha T+wAM rdo rje'o//
16b5(rgyud): sku la mi yi rus pas rgyan//klu rnams skrag par byed pa po//skyil bkrungs phyed pa'i gar gyis bzhugs//gcer bu 'jigs byed rang bzhin te//zhal gyi mche bcud thad gtsigs//dbu skra ser zhing 
16b6(rgyud): gyen du 'bar//thod pas spyi bo rgyan byas pa//sa 'og bdun ni 'khrug par byed//dpa' bo de ltar bsan par bya//phyis ni phogs kyi 'dang mo la//oM dar hUM dang khaM dang swa//yi ge yongs su gyur 
16b7(rgyud): pa las//rnal 'byor ma ni rnam pa bzhi//sna tshogs sdim pa dod can ma//sa 'og gnas ma rab bzang ma//lha mo de dag mdog sngon mo bkrags chen zhal gcig phyag bzhi ma//ha ma'i 
17a1(rgyud): @#//dkyil 'khor rnam par bzhugs//g.yon brkyang ba yis zhabs kyis bzhugs//g.yas na gri gug thod pa 'dzin//g.yon na kha T+wAM cang te'u//rus rgyan lha mo kun 'jigs rma//
17a2(rgyud): zhal gyi mche ba cung zad gtsigs//stag gi lpags pa'i na bza' banabs//skra ni ser po gyen du 'bar//thod pa'i phreng bas spyi bo rgyan//da rnams mgo rgyu'i sa phreng ba can//lha mo'i 'od zer spros gyur 
17a3(rgyud): pas//klu rnams skrag par mdzad ma mo//phyogs kyi 'dab bzhugs cher bzhad ma'o//blo ldan mtshams kyi 'dab bzang la//nyi ma bzhi la hUM yig bzhi//hUM yig so sor gyur pa las//chang 'tshong ma dang 
17a4(rgyud): 'byung po ma//bzhin bzang nam mkhar 'gro ma ste//mtshams kyi 'dab mar gnas lha mo//kha dog ser mo grags chen mo//thams cad na bza' ser po bsgoms//g.yas brkyang ba yi zhabs kyi 
17a5(rgyud): gnas//zhal gcig ma la phyag bzhi ma//klu brgyad rgyan byas lha mo dang //g.yas na gri gug cang te'u 'dzin//g.yon na kha T+wAM thod pa'o//sku las 'od zer ser po 'phro//shin tu sgeg pa'i nyams dang 
17a6(rgyud): ldan//cung zad mche ba gcig pa'i zhal//thams cad gar byed skra ser ma//klu rnams skrag pa skyed byed ma//mtshams kyi cha ru rnam par gnas//de nas bsam par bya ba ni//slob dpon he ru kas 
17a7(rgyud): rang gi lus las skyes pa'i 'od kyis phyogs bcur bzhugs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa rnams la yang dag par reg par byas nas slar log ste de bzhin gshegs pa rnam sa la yang dag par rig 
17b1(rgyud): pa'i 'od zer las lha mo'i tshogs yang dag par spros pas he ru ka dang /rnal 'byor brgyad lhan cig tu bkrus so//bkrus pa tsam gyis de bzhin gshegs pa rnams cod pan du gyur to//dkyil 'khor 
17b2(rgyud): rdzogs par mthong nas ni//slob dpon bdag nyid chen po yis//phyis ni gtor ma btang bar bya//khyad par du ni 'o ma'o//sangs rgyas thod pa'i sbyor ldan pa'i//shes rab can gyis tshang btung zhing /
17b3(rgyud): mar me bzhi ni dbul bar bya//'bras bu bzhi yang dbul bya zhing //de nas slob ma blo chen gyis//ras zung gsum ni dbul bar bya//der ni gdan yang btabs par bya//slob dpon bdag nyid 
17b4(rgyud): chen po yis//de ru rnal 'byor ma 'chad dang //blo gros chen pa thugs dgyes pas//sna tshogs zas dang sna tshogs skom//dpa' bo rnal 'byor ma tshim bya//mgu bar sems pa'i yid kyis ni//'khor 
17b5(rgyud): lo khyab par che bya ste//phyis ni slob dpon brtse chen bdag/phyi rol sa ru byung nas ni//bde ba yi ni gdan bzhugs te//slob mas maN+Dal gsum byas la//bla ma me tog sna tshogs mchod//
17b6(rgyud): zhabs la sbyi gtugs 'di skad gsol//ston pa bcom ldan bla ma kye//sems can don la gcig brtson pa//nam mkha' lding bzang sbyor ba dang //sha ra ka ye sbyor ba 'tshal//dus su zin 
17b7(rgyud): pa bzlog pa yi//sems can don ni bzang po 'tshal//sangs rgyas thod pa'i rnal 'byor pa//ston pa chen pos de dag gsungs//bdag gi the tsom bzlog pa dang //sems can don ni mthar thug 
18a1(rgyud): @#//bgyi//brtan pa dang ni rgyu ba dang /yan lag skyes dang bcos ma yi//dug rnams dug med par bgyi zhing //sems can thams cad 'tsho bgyi ba//bla ma chen po de dag 
18a2(rgyud): gsungs//de nas slob dpon brtse ba yis//byin gyis brlabs pa'i thugs kyis ni//khrag 'thung go 'phang gnas pa yi//slob ma la ni so shing sbyin//so shing sngags ni sbyin par bya//
18a3(rgyud): oM hUM he b+ha ga ba tI mu khaM pa ri shud+d+he ku ru swAhA//lan bdun mngon par sngags byas la//so bdud nas ni kha bshal te//slob ma lhag par gnas par bya//rang gi lag pa byug bya zhing //me 
18a4(rgyud): tog gis kyang bkri bar bya//mi gran pa lag par zla ba bsam//zla ba'i zer gyi phyogs bcu yi//sangs rgyas spyan drangs gdon mi za//sangs rgyas tshogs kun zla bar blta//grags chen 
18a5(rgyud): thams cad rab zhugs shing //zla ba zhu bar gdon mi za//slob dpon bsgoms de'i nus pa yis//slob ma'i mgo ru lag bzhag ste//lhag par gnas pa'i gsang sngags bzlas//
18a6(rgyud): oM sarba bud+d+ha a tra a mu /s+ya kA ya ma d+hiShaTaHa na/yan+tu ma tsa la ma tsa la tiShaTaHa tiShaTaHa ha ra ha ra/bi d+ha ra bi d+ha ra/d+ha ra d+ha ra/tra pa tra pa/b+ho b+ha ga bAM ta sarba bud+d+ha ka pA la badzra sa ga taM ma la 
18a7(rgyud): ya shrI g+h+r-i ku ru ku ru hUM hUM ha ha//sngags 'dis lan bdun du bzlas pas lhag par gnas par bya'o//sangs rgyas de dag thams cad kyis de'i lus la yang dag par byin gyis rlob par 'gyur 
18b1(rgyud): ro//lhag par gnas pa tsam gyis rdzu 'phrul chen po'i klu rnams skrag car dug gi bum pa yang 'dor ro//nor rgyas la sogs pa'i klu stobs po che rnams dpyod par 'gyur ro//e ma'o sangs rgyas 
18b2(rgyud): kyis gsungs pa'i//lhag par gnas pa tsams gyis ni//'di lta'i sdug bsngal thob 'gyur na//mkha' lding rtog pa'i sems can gyis//bdag ni ji ltar 'gro bar 'gyur//de ma thag tu sleb 
18b3(rgyud): dpon gyis//dkyil 'khor nang du me tog ni//rnam pa sna tshogs dbul bar bya//sna tshogs spos dang lha ba shos dbul//dung chos zangs chos snod dag ni//man ji'i steng du bzhag par bya//
18b4(rgyud): mchod yon gyi ni don ched du//bum pa rnams la lhag gnas bya//khrag 'thung go 'phangs gnas pa yis//rnal 'byor ma brgyad bskor ba yi//khrag 'thung rnam pa spyan drangs te//dkyil 'khor 
18b5(rgyud): ji bzhin yang dag ldan//mdog dang mtshan mar ldan pa yi//de bzhin gshegs rnams sa bon gyi//'od zer gyi ni rnam par bsam//bdag nyid chen po grags chen po//bum pa ru ni zhugs tsam 
18b6(rgyud): gyis//dam tshig dkyil 'khor zhu bar gyur//rnal 'byor ldan pa'i sems dang ni//slob dpon gyis ni 'di ltar blta//de nas zungs ma'i snod du chu blugs shing dung phor chus bkang ste//man ji'i 
18b7(rgyud): steng du bzhag par bya'o//yang phyir byung nas slob ma'i don du bdag nyid kyis ras kyi mig dgab par bya'o//ras gsum gan par gyur pa'i slob ma phyis//dkyil 'khor gyi sgor bzhag la chus gtor 
19a1(rgyud): @#//te sngags bzla'o//a b+hiShiny+tsa tu mAM sarba yo gi ni ma ku Ta a b+hi taN+Di ra yaM tu a tsi du tUM swAhA//tsa da ban byin gyis brlabs pa'i sngags so//de nas slob dpon gyis kye khyod 
19a2(rgyud): su zhes smra'o//slob mas bdag ni rgyal ba'i sras smra'o//de'i rjes la dkyil 'khor gyi dbus su me tog la ni gsum du gtor nas//mig ras bsal te/dkyil 'khor mchod par bya'o//
19a3(rgyud): oM he ru ka badzra puSh+pa yaM pra tI ts+tsha hUM/dbus su'o//oM tsi tra se nA puSh+pa yaM pra ti ts+tsha hUM/shar du'o//oM kA mi nA puSh+pa yaM pa tA ts+tsha hUM//lho yi tsau//oM pA tA la bA si ni puSh+pa yaM pra tIts+tsha hUM/
19a4(rgyud): nub tu'o//oM su b+ha dre puSh+pa yaM pra tI ts+tsa hUM/byang du'o//oM shau Di ni puSh+pa yaM pra tU ts+tsha hUM/mer ro//oM bud+d+ha ni puSh+pa yaM pra tits+tsha hUM/bden bril du'o//oM tsa tu ra b+ha tra puSh+pa 
19a5(rgyud): ye pra tI ts+tsha hUM/rlung du'o//oM A kA sha bA si ni puSh+pa yaM pa tits+tsha hUM//dbang ldan du'o//de nas dkyil 'khor gyi lha'i sku mdog ji lta bu dang mtshan ma la sogs pa de bzhin du 
19a6(rgyud): slob ma la bstan par bya'o//yang dung phor blangs te chu btung bar bya'o//de 'thungs pa tsam gyis klu thams cad skrag cing skyobs dug skyug par 'gyur ro//brtan pa dang g.yon pa'i dug 
19a7(rgyud): gang yin pa dang /gzhan yang sbyar ba dang /nye bar sbyar ba'i dug gang yin pa dang /mda' dug dang /byug ba'i dug dang /dgra rnams kyis gang sbyor ba dang //bud med rnams kyis gang 
19b1(rgyud): sbyar ba'i drug de rnams nye bar 'pho bar 'gyur ro//gal te klu yis zin pa yang //de ru de ni nges par 'gro//gzhan yang sbrul gyi dug kyang rung //chu ni 'thungs pa tsam gyis kyang //de dag thams cad 
19b2(rgyud): 'chi bar 'gyur//de yi phyir na dung gi chu//'di ru btung bar the tsom med//de nas slob ma dri bar bya//'dir ni sha li kar byed cing //mkha' lding shes pa'i slob dpon 'gyur//bsam pa rnam 
19b3(rgyud): par dag shes nas//phyis ni bkas gnang sbyin par bya//stong ngam brgya ru gsol ba gdab//de nas sngags kyi man ngag sbyin//ji srid sbrul gyis zin dus dang //ji srid mkha' lding rigs yod 
19b4(rgyud): par//de srid de dang de las kyang //ji srid dus su zin med par//sangs rgyas thod pa'i rnal 'byor pas//de srid 'bad rtsol bya ba min//dus zin 'chi ba'ang shes bya ste//nam mkha' lding 
19b5(rgyud): gi bya ba bya//dus su zin no zhes smra ba//de srid rnal 'byor che 'di zhes//de ru las ni brtsam par bya//bdag nyid he ru kar gyur cing //rnal 'byor bam brgyad yang dag ldan//blo ldan sngon gsungs 
19b6(rgyud): gsang sngags kyis//phyis ni gtor ma sbyin par bya//de rjes dkyil 'khor byas nas ni//sngon du gsungs pa'i gsang sngags kyis//gnas dgu ru ni mchod par bya//mkhas pa yis ni mchod byas 
19b7(rgyud): rjes//mnyan par gzhag pas las rnams brtsam//gang du rdzu 'phrul che klu yod//de ru sems bzang 'od zer spro//de rnams kyi ni snying bskams pa//ci zhig bgyi'o zhes ni smra//gsungs pa/
20a1(rgyud): @#//lha mo sna tshogs sde ma yis//sangs rgyas thod par sngon du gsungs//kye kye grags pa che ba'i klu//song ste 'di dus zin gyur pa'i//dus zin 'dzin dang bzung ba med//
20a2(rgyud): dug med sbrul yang med pa'i//gang phyir dogs pas rmongs gyur pa//de phyir skye bo rnams 'chir 'gyur//de nas 'od zer slar log ste//snying ka ru ni thim par 'gyur//yang ni sems ni 
20a3(rgyud): mnyam gzhag pa'i//slob dpon gyis ni ngo mtshar bstan//de nas sa la ma lhung lci ba blangs te mdab chags ri skegs gnyis bya zhing snod dkar pa byas te//ro bsregs pa'i sol ba bzhag 
20a4(rgyud): par bya'o//ro bsregs pa'i sol pa la snag tsha byas te//sems can gyi don rig pas dben par sngags bri bar bya'o//oM bud+d+ha ka pA li ni hri tad tha ba ya b+ho sha li ka na a na ya nA bud+d+ha 
20a5(rgyud): ka pA la s+ya Adz+nyA ma laM g+ha ya hUM hUM sa raM tu sarba da DI ka pha ti ka tse ta/maM d+ha g+hi di sarba dzi i na su naM ti pa ya ga re re kar ko Ta ka pra b+he ta ya nA ga rA dza na spu taM ti murda na re re sa spyad a ba sa 
20a6(rgyud): spye ma tsa la ha ma bu la ha dza aM tsa la ha bul ha ro ra b+ha ru kri pra b+hi ta ya na ga rA dza na u ha puT Ta mu d+hi pra na dza i zhes pa ni sha li ka' snying po'i sngags so//de nas blo chen rnal 'byor pas//
20a7(rgyud): sems ni shin tu mnyam bzhag pas//yongs rdzogs sha li ka byas la//pho dang mo ru brtag par bya//snag tsha yis ni bri bar bya//mig gi sngon du mi bzhag cing //sha li ka bzang pos 
20b1(rgyud): phyogs blta//de nas lcug ma sngags kyis bsngags so//oM haM ha la khyab 'jug gi sa bon no//oM baM tshangs pa'i sa bon no//oM saM zhes bya ba ni zhi byed kyi sa bon no//oM ha ha ha ha ha haM swAhA/
20b2(rgyud): mngag pa'i sngags so//'di lta bu yi sngags kyi mchog/stong gsum na yang rnyed par dka'//lan bdun bsngags pa tsam gyis ni//'gro bar 'dir ni khre tsom med//klu rnams skrag par byed 
20b3(rgyud): pa yi//mkha' lding nyams dga' song nas ni//klu yi gnas su zhugs de'i che//mkha' lding bye ba'i stobs dang ldan//rings nas kyang ni bshog rlung gis//stabs po che yi klu rnams skrag/
20b4(rgyud): mtho ris mi yul sa 'og dang //gang yang gzhan na gnas pa dang //gzhan yang rung ste de thams cad//skrag nas ci bgyi zhes ni smra//klu gang gis zin pa de gal te yang shin/bdag nyid chen po sha 
20b5(rgyud): yi ka mthong ma thag long bar 'gyur ro//klu dang sprul de dag gis sha ka la 'di skad ces gsol to//kya sha li ka gzhan du gsung bar ma mdzad cig/de ltar thos nas sha li ka mgu ba'i 
20b6(rgyud): sems kyis dug gis zin pa'i mdun du sprul long pa khyer te 'jog go/de nas slab dpon gyis bstas te/sta'i gong du blangs la sha li ka mngag pa'i sngags kyis sprul long ba la bsnun no/
20b7(rgyud): skad cig de nyid la de'i mig dbye bar 'dir som nyid la med do//dug gis zin pa 'gyal te shi ba sbrul gyis mthong nas de'i las la 'gro ba 'ong bar byed pas dus su zin pa dug med par 
21a1(rgyud): @#//'gyur zhing //dga' ba'i sems kyis ldang ngo//zin pa langs pa tsam nyid na sha li kas sbrul bzung ste mi snang bar 'gyur ro//skad cig ma de nyid la slob dpon gyis 
21a2(rgyud): stong pa nyid kyi ye shes bsgom par bya'o//stong pa dri med kun rang bzhin//chos rnams stong pa'i rang bzhin kye//gang gis 'dzin cing gang zhig 'dzin//mtha' dag sgyu ma'i rang bzhin kyi dngos po 
21a3(rgyud): ma lus pa mtha' dag dang dug kyang stong pa nyid du sangs rgyas thod pa'i rgyud las gsungs te/zhag nyi shu rtsa gcig gi mthar thug par ji srid du lus ma zhig pa de srid du slob dpon gyis yang 
21a4(rgyud): dag par brtsam par bya/de ltar slob ma la rjes su gnang bar byin nas phyis lung bstan par bya'o//des lan brgya'am stong du gsol ba btab nas//dug 'joms pa'i sngags bstan par 
21a5(rgyud): bya'o||oM bud+d+ha ka pA li ni AHhUM//dug bza' ba'i sngags so//de nas gzhan yang don du gnyer ba'i sems can rnams thams cad la dkyil 'khor bstan te/dang por gyi chu 'thungs pa 
21a6(rgyud): des ni dug med par 'gyur te/sbrul gyis mi 'dzin no//gal te 'dzin na de'i tshe klu 'chi bar 'dir the tsom med/de ltar slob ma sha li ka yi sbyor ba la mngon par brtson pa la bka' gnang 
21a7(rgyud): ba byin te/grong gang du gnas der dug gis 'dzin pa med do//gal te yong na de'i klu 'chi bar 'gyur ro//de ltar thos nas slob ma rdo rje'i sbyor ba dang ldan pa'i sems kyis gang ci mdzad 
21b1(rgyud): rung ba'i yon der dbul bar bya'o//de nas phyi rol gyi sar byung ste//gtor ma btang bar bya'o//de nas slob dpon gyis dkyil 'khor gyi sngags kyis gshegs su gsol to//oM bud+d+ha ka pA li naM

21b3(rgyud): zin pa 'tsho bar 'gyur//nyi ma bdun du 'chi ba dang //gzhan yang dug gis nyams pa rnams//chu ni byin pa tsam gyis ni//'di ru 'tsho bar the tshom med//sngags 'dis dkyil 'khor 
21b4(rgyud): byas nas ni//gnas dgu rnams su mchod par bya//dung phor chu yis bkang byas pa//dkyil 'khor dbus su bzhag par bya//de nas brtse ba chen po yi//bdag nyid rnal 'byor pa yis ni//
21b5(rgyud): rang gi lag pa g.yon pa la//zla ba yi ni khyad par bsam//bcu drug cha las yongs rdzogs pa'i//dri med dge ba'i lus can te//de'i dbus sa bon mi shigs dge/kha dog dkar po'i lus 
21b6(rgyud): can no//lag des snod kyi kha bcad de//de nas gsang sngags bzla bar bya'o//oM sarba bi Sha ha ra ha ra hUM hUM phaT phaT swAhA//sngags 'dis lan bdun gyi bar du gnas mngon par bsngags te/
21b7(rgyud): mi shigs pa dang lhan cig zla ba zhu bar gyur nas chu'i nang du thim ste/chu dang zla ba gcig tu 'dres pas shin tu dkar por 'gyur ro//gal te dug gis zin pa shin de'i tshe chu blud do//
22a1(rgyud): @#//yang na shi ba yin nam go nas rkang pa'i mthar thug par lan gsum du chu rgyun mi 'chad par blugs na langs par 'dir the tshom med do//de nas chu blud nas rnal 'byor mar 
22a2(rgyud): 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas thod pa'i rgyud rnal 'byor ma'i gong na med pa gsang ba'i yang shin tu gsang ba zhes bya ba las sha li ka'i sbyor ba dang /chu'i sbyor ba'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o////
22a3(rgyud): de nas yang dag rab bshad pa//shin tu rnyed dka'i bcud len le'u//de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying po mchog/rnal 'byor ma yi gong na med//gzhon nu ma dang sha ri ka/grub ma byis pa can 
22a4(rgyud): ma dang //de bzhin thod bzang can ma'o//'di dag sman ni chen po lnga//sangs rgyas lngas kyang rnyed par dka'//'di dag zos pa tsam gyis ni//bskal pa bye bar de 'tsho'o//gzhon nu ma ni zla 
22a5(rgyud): drug tu//de bzhin sha ri ka zla gsum//grub ma zla ba gcig tu'o//byis can ma ni zla phyed gnyis//thod pa can yang zla bar te//tshe 'phel ba yi mchog yin no//bya rog ma ni bya rog gzugs 
22a6(rgyud): glang po ma ni ngang pa'i gzugs//drod ma drod kyi gzugs can te//sngo bsangs dkar mo sngon mo'i mig/kha sbyar sbyor ba'i dus su ni//mig las byung ba'i rdzas gang yin//sman de'i ming ni 
22a7(rgyud): gzhon nu ma//'di ru bstan par so ma nyi med//de yi padam las byung rdzas//rtsa yi nang na rnam par gnas//gsar sbas pa'i spyod yul te//sman de sha ri ka ru gsungs//me tog ldan pa'i 
22b1(rgyud): bud med gang //kha dog nag mos rab mdzes ma//gang du thabs shes gnyis 'dus pa//de yi kha sbyar sbyor las byung //sman de'i ming ni grub mar bshad//sangs rgyas thong pa'i sbyor ba yis//bud 
22b2(rgyud): med rab mdzes ma nyams dang //dbang po gnyis kyi sbyor ba yis//mi phyed nye bar spyad pa yi//dol dang por byung ba'i rdzas//sman de byis pa can no zhes//bdag nyid chen po'i lha mos gsungs//
22b3(rgyud): lhan cig skyes dga' byung ba'i rdzas//byang chub sems mchog chen mar khu//chags pa yi chu dag dang ni//bsre 'gyur 'di ni the tsom med//sman de'i ming ni thod can ma'o//zla ba nyi ma'i sbyor ba'i sa//
22b4(rgyud): bya bar 'dir ni the tshom med//zla bas zla bar nyi mas nyir//de lus skra dkar gnyer ma zhi//ji srid sangs rgyas bzhugs kyi bar//de yi tshe ni sangs rgyas 'gyur//sman 'di grub pa tsam gyis ni//
22b5(rgyud): dag pa'i lus su 'byung bar 'gyur//dngos po gang dang gang yang rung //lcags dang zangs pa'i snod dag la//dngul ni byugs pa tsam gyis ni//dngul dang gser du 'gyur ba yin//de nas yang dag 
22b6(rgyud): rab bshad pa//dgongs bshad shin tu rnyed dka' ba//rgyud gzhan du ni rab sbas pa//'bad pas de 'di nga rab gsal ba//dri chu ye shes rdo rje ste//dri chen rdo rje bzang po nyid//khrag ni mar me rdo rje 
22b7(rgyud): yin//khu ba rdo rje'i thabs byung nyid//sha ni chos kyi rdo rje yin//de lnga rnal 'byor ma yi mchog/gang gi dam tshig rnyed dka' ba'i//thabs 'dir bdag gis bshad par bya//ba lang sha ni 'bar ba ste//
23a1(rgyud): @#//khyi yi sha la spo bar gsungs//rta ya shal bza' bar bshad//glang pa'i shal zhu bar gsungs//sha chen la ni bzang por bshad//de lnga rnal 'byor ma chag tu gsungs/
23a2(rgyud): rnal 'byor ma la de ldan par//gal te gcig tu byed nus pa//de tshe rnam rtog de thams cad//'jig 'gyur 'dir ni the tshom med//zan la bsed nams zhes bya ste//chod mal ni sdig pa nyid//rnal 
23a3(rgyud): 'byor shin tu rnyed dka' gsungs//byis pa la ni 'gal ba nyid//g.yungs mo la ni bzang mor gsungs//rus pa'i phreng la rdzas zhes pa//skyes bu la ni rdo rjer gsungs//thud med la ni snod 
23a4(rgyud): bzang nyid//dbang po gnyis kyi rab sbyor la//gzi brjid phreng chen de bshad do//rab chags shin tu mthong ba nyid//'khyud pa la ni chos zhes brjod//'dri ba la ni mar che'o//shin tu 'di la 
23a5(rgyud): me zhes bya//'gro ba la ni phreng can ma//'od la shin tu phreng can ma//myur ba la ni byug ces bya//dpa' ba la ni bzang de brjod//padam rtsa 'jo phreng can ma//sngags kyis mtshan la rab nges zhe'o//
23a6(rgyud): ldag la lte 'og ces bya ste//so la bza' ba sna la phad//mig la rol rtsed mu tig rna//gsang ba'i yul la rnyed dka' zhe'o//'di dag bshad do lag chen po//'jig rten gsum na rab rnyed 
23a7(rgyud): dka'//sangs rgyas thod pa'i rnal 'byor pa//med par phan tshun smra mi bya//'on kyang bshad chen de nyid 'di//gcig la gcig gi smra bar bya//rgyud rnams thams cad du bsam pa//rgyud 
23b1(rgyud): 'di ru ni bsal bar byas//nye 'dun grong mtha' lhan cig zhe'o//'dus pa phyi rol zhes su gsungs//nye ba'i 'dus pa nang de bzhin//gnas la 'bras bu zhes su gsungs//nye 'dun grong mtha'?
23b2(rgyud): nyid do//grong mtha' la ni sa bon gsungs//nye ba'i grong mtha' myu gu'o//tshogs pa la ni sdong bur gsungs//gcig pu la ni 'dab ma ste//stong pa la ni rtsa bzhi'o//de ltar dgongs pas bshad pa 
23b3(rgyud): ni//bde gshegs rnams kyis mi mkhyen no//spyan ma la sogs rnal 'byor ma//rnams kyi khong du mi chud cing //byang chub sems dpa'i yul min zhing //bla ma gus pa'i rnal 'byor pas//
23b4(rgyud): spros pa thams cad spangs nas ni//rdzas la chags pa mi bya'o//shin tu stong sgom rnal 'byor pa//'dod chags zhe sdang phrag dog med//gti mug dang ni nga rgyal spangs//nyon mongs thams cad rnam 
23b5(rgyud): par spangs//za 'thung de bzhin glu len na//shin tu spros pa spang bar bya//bde bas dga' ba nyid dang ni//shes rab thabs kyis bdag nyid gsungs//bla med de nyid zla phyed kyis//'grub par the tshom 
23b6(rgyud): med pa'o//kye lags grogs mi 'gro kun gang //mtha' dag dri ma med pa yi//blta dang bral la skye ba ltos//gsog gsob rang bzhin brdzun slu ba//pad ma grib kha lta bur ltos//dbang po lnga ni sgyu 
23b7(rgyud): 'dra zhing //lnga phung dag pa'i rang bzhin gyi//rnal 'byor 'di ni bsgoms pas 'grub//sangs rgyas thod pa'i rnal 'byor ma'i gong na med pa gsang ba'i yang shin tu gsang ba zhes bya ba las smin 
24a1(rgyud): @#//bzang po dang /dgongs pas bshad pa'i le'u ste lnga pa'o////
24b1(rgyud): nan tan//rgod cing pad+ma'i spyan dang ldan//zla ba'i cha 'dra gdong mdzes ma//mu cor mi smra brtan zhir phul//des na slob ma blo chen po//shin tu dang pa'i sems dang ldan//'dod med mang po dul khe 
24b2(rgyud): dang //chags dang chags bral rnam par spangs//sems can don la shin tu sbyor//gti mug zhe sdang phrag dog spangs//sangs rgyas kun gyis byin gyis brlabs//de rnams la ni de nyid

25a1(rgyud): @#//gzhan gar bkod kye//su yis sangs rgyas spyan drangs kye//'gro ba brdzun 'dra'i som nyir ltung //mtha' dag stong pa gos med kye//chu nang zla ba ci 'dra ba//de

25a3(rgyud): gti mug las ni bskyed pa skyes//sangs rgyas thod pa'i rgyud rnal 'byor ma'i gong na med pa gsang ba'i yang shin tu gsang ba las dkyil 'khor 'jigs pa bri ba dang //slob ma

25a5(rgyud): gnas su 'am//'on te rgya mtsho'i 'gram dag tu//phyi yi dkyil 'khor bla med bri//rdul chen sngon po ni ro bsregs pa'i sol ba las bya'o//rdul tshon dmar po ni dur khrod 
25a6(rgyud): kyi so phag las bya'o//rdul tshon ljang ku ni nam ba'i 'dab ma las bya'o//rdul tshon dkar po ni mi'i rus pa'i phye ma las bya'o//rdul tshod ser po ni yung ba'i phye ma las bya'o//

25b1(rgyud): lham pa yi//sgo bzhir rta babs kyis rgyan pa//ba dan rnam pa bzhi yis rgyan//dra ba dra ba phyed pas spras//phyi rol khag byer dag pa dang //rin chen pha gur yang dag ldan//pad+ma nye

25b6(rgyud): mgur dang ni 'khor lo dang //rkang gdub dang ni dpung rgyan dang //gdu bu rked pa'i ska rags dang //dpa' bo rna cha mchog gis rgyan//'di 'dra'i gzugs can gtso bo ni//hUM zhes zlos par byed

26a1(rgyud): @#//'chang //so so yi ni ming ji bzhin//de rnams ming ni rab tu bshad//phreng bzang ma/'dod can ma/'jigs ma/thub dka' ma/dge ba'i ska rags can ma/

26b2(rgyud): snyan mnan na//ci phyir khyod ni mi dgyes gyur//gal te khyod bdag tshig gsan na//mi dgyes gyur pa mtha' dag thong //srid 'jigs skye bo kun lhung bas//stong pa'i thugs ni ma mdzad

27a1(rgyud): @#//bsreg mi bya zhing //'bras dang 'bru rnams kyis kyang min//blo ldan gal te zhi 'dod na//me tog gis ni sbyin bsreg bya//me tog lnga po mchog med dgos//
27a2(rgyud): 'o mar yang dag ldan pas bya//gal te zhi ba'i sbyin sreg 'dod//zhi ba'i gzugs sogs ldan pa yis//zhi ba'i thab khung gru bzhi pa//zab dang rgyar ni mtho gang bra//la la rdo rje he lnga

29a1(rgyud): @#//thur du kha bltam shing //kha nam khrag ni skyug pa nyid//sha yi dbus su bsam par bya//seng ldeng sol bas me bya ste//med na 'ba' 'bol nyid kyi//'ba' 'bo 
29a2(rgyud): tsher mas sha phug ste//brgya rtsa brgyad du sngags kyis sreg/oM mA ra ya mA ra ya kA li ya kA li ya sarba sha truM nA sha ya hUM phaT//bdag nyid he ru kar gyur cing //sku mdog sngon po thugs 
29a3(rgyud): 'khrugs pas//dgra rnams thams cad brlag par ni//skad cig de la the tshom med//phreng mdzad 'jigs shing 'jigs par byed//ha ha zhes mdzad 'jigs par mdzad//
29a4(rgyud): gdug pa thams cad gsad par ni//sbyin sreg tsam gyis the tsom med//'di lta'i bsad pa'i sbyin sreg dang //mngon spyod kyang ni rnyed dka' ba//rnal 'byor lha mos rtogs min na//
29a5(rgyud): sngags skyes phal lta smos ci dgos//de phyir sangs rgyas thod pa yi//rnal 'byor ma rgyal dang bral bas//shin tu mnyam par gzhag pa'i sems//lha rnams kyis kyang rnyed par dka'//
29a6(rgyud): ljang ku'i chu yis me zhi bya//gang gis bsregs shing gang zhig bsregs//gang gi zhi ba gang zhig/gang gis rnam rtog kun bcom pa//de yis sems ni mgu bar byed//sangs rgyas 
29a7(rgyud): thod pa zhes bya ba'i rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud bla na med pa gsang ba'i yang shin tu gsang ba las/sbyin sreg ga cho ga'i le'u ste brgyad pa'o////
29b1(rgyud): spyod pa'i le'u shin tu rnyed dka' ba//bud med shin tu mchog gyur dang //rnal 'byor gang gis spyod kun byed//dngos grub brgyad dang yang dag ldan//de nas spyod pa yang dag brtsam//sems can 
29b2(rgyud): dngos grub gang gang 'dod//de de'i dngos grub rtag tu ster//lag pas thod pa bzung byas te//gcer bu skra grol gyur pa yi//brtul zhugs can gyi spyod pas 'khyam//gang du gang tshe de 
29b3(rgyud): tshe der//rtag tu spyod pa thams cad bya//rgyan rnams kun las rnam grol zhing //nam mkha' dang mtshungs gyur pa yi//shes rab can gyis khyim gyim du slongs mo bya bar the tshom med//
29b4(rgyud): yid dang 'thun par kun blangs nas//bsam gtan gnas su the tshom med//dga' ba'i sems kyis bsams nas ni//rnal 'byor ma yi slongs mo bslangs//chos la gzhol ba'i rnal 'byor 
29b5(rgyud): pas//thod pa'i steng du bzhag pa yi//rdzas ni de nas bza' bar bya//rdzas ni zos ma thag tu yang //khyim khyim thal phung shing drung dang //gang du gang tshe der de'i tshe yi//gang gis dga' ba'i 
29b6(rgyud): sems de yis//blo gros chen po gnyid log 'phreng //'di lta'i spyod pa'i sbyor ba yi//phyag rgya chen po nges par thob//de ltar yang ni rgyud las gsungs//gang du ji ltar der de ltar//gang gis 
29b7(rgyud): de yis sangs rgyas te//rang gi rnam rtog dag gis nyams//mtha' dag rang bzhin rnam par dag/sangs rgyas thod pa zhes bya ba rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud bla na med pa gsang ba'i yang shin tu

30b1(rgyud): pa ste//de yang snod bzang mchog la 'grub//byis pa rmongs pas mi shes so//bdag don gcig pu la 'bab cing //kye ma gcig tu dka' 'bad pas//ji ltar sems can don 'grub 'gyur//
30b2(rgyud): snod bzang la ni gnas pa'i bcud//ze 'bru gsang ba'i spyod yul te//ro bcud rtsa yi nang gnas pa'i//sman chen re bcud ma ru brjod//gang gis lcags ni gser 'gyur ba//gcig gis kyang ni gangs 
30b3(rgyud): med 'gyur//'di lta bu yi sman chen mchog/ro bcud ma ni de bzhin gnas//rab 'byor cho ga sngon 'gro bas//sman gyi mchog ni 'grub par 'gyur//der na yang dag sbyor ba bshad//
30b4(rgyud): dad pa brtan pa yis ni nyon//me tog ldan pa'i pad med gang //gzhon nu kha dog nag mo ste//rgyud 'di thos pa dang ldan zhing //ngo tsha med cing ltos med la//'dod pa chung zhing sems can brtse//
30b5(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa rnams brten cing //rtag tu rab sbas rtsog med ma//'di lta'i bud med rab blangs te//sman chen mchog ni yang dag brtsam//skyes bu shin tu chags che zhing //myur du byang 
30b6(rgyud): chub sems 'bab pa//gang yin de la dngos grub med//'di lta bu yi sman chenapo//sangs rgyas kyis kyang rang rnyed dka'//shin tu brtan pa'i sems kyis ni//blo ldan bud med de rnams brten//
30b7(rgyud): de ltar yang gsungs pa//rab tu brtan pa gtso bo'i gnas//zhes ni sgrub pa po rnams smra//de phyir brtan pa'i sems kyis ni////snod bzang 'bras bu bskul bar bya//de bzhi me tog ldan bud 
31a1(rgyud): @#//med//gser gyi sbyor ba rnyed dka' brtsam//grub pa'i bcud kyis byugs pa yi//zla ba rang gi snying kar bsam//sngon du ri yi rgyal pos rgyun//der ni yi ge hrI sa bon//
31a2(rgyud): sems can don la cig tu 'bad//blo ldan dgos pa yang dgu brtsam//zangs ma'i dngos po srang gcig bcas//zlum po snod bzang la bcug ste//g.yon pa bskum pa yi g.yas zur bzhag/med na 
31a3(rgyud): lcags kyang bya ba yin//brtan pa'i sbyor ba'i sems kyis ni//de nas blo ldan las rnams brtsam//ri rab zla ba sa bon rgyal//zhu 'gyur the tshom med pa yin//snod bzang der ni ngal bsos pas 
31a4(rgyud): 'pho ba'i sbyor bas 'dzag pa nyid//bzang por myur du rab blangs te//mkhas pa yis ni byug par bya//rdo rje blo byugs pa tsam gyis ni//me yi nang du gzhug bya zhing //de nas shes rab can 
31a5(rgyud): bltas na//gser du 'gyur bar the tshom med//de ni ma sha gcig gis ni//srang brgya ru ni 'gyur ba yin//de ltar gser gyi sbyor ba mchog/'jig rten gsum na'ang rnyed par dka'/
31a6(rgyud): gal te sman chen tshad min na//de tshe sangs rgyas tshad ma yin//rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po 'dir//dngos po gang dang gang gsungs pa//sangs rgyas thob pa'i sbyor ba yis//de dag thams 
31a7(rgyud): cad bden par 'gyur//gang zhig 'di tshad min smra ba'i//blo gros chen po thos ldan pa//ji srid sangs rgyas bzhugs kyi bar//de dag thams cad dmyal bar 'gro//sman 'di tsam gyis 
31b1(rgyud): ma grub na//chos phung brgyad khri bzhi stong dang //bla med rnal 'byor ma rnams kyi//de dag thams cad brdzun par 'gyur//mtshams med lnga ni byed pa dang //sems can sangs rgyas chos la 'tsho//
31b2(rgyud): sman gyi mchog ni 'grub 'gyur ba//skad cig de la the tshom med//sems can don la gcig tu 'bad//sgom ldan 'grub pa lta ci smos//dman mchog gser dang dngul du 'gyur//ji srid 
31b3(rgyud): sangs rgyas ye shes yod//mi ni bskal pa bye bar 'tsho//de nas yang dag rab bshad pa//mo sham rnams kyi bu med pa//'ang dag sbyor bas bkrus tsam gyis//skad cig de la bu ldan 'gyur 
31b4(rgyud): sngon gyi gtso bos ji skad gsungs//khrus kyi cho ga rab tu bshad//kha dog lnga yi rdul tshon nam//yang na rdul tshon dkar po yis//dpag dga' skrag par byed pa yis//dkyil 'khor 
31b5(rgyud): 'jigs pa bri bar bya//der ni bum pa lta che ba//zlum po bzhi ni bzhag par bya//shes rab can gyis chu yis dgang //ras kyis kyang ni dkri bar bya//rang gi khrag gis mtshan ma bri//bud med 
31b6(rgyud): mo shan de yi ni//khams kyi gtso bo blangs nas ni//mkhas pas bum pa'i kha la rgyur//de nas khrag 'thung go 'phang la//gnas pa sgom pa'i mchog ldan pas//nag po dmar mo 'jigs chen 
31b7(rgyud): po//rdo rje hUM las yang dag byung //'jigs pa'i me ltar 'jigs byed pa//zhal cig pa la phyag bzhi pa//bla ma 'jigs pa'i gar byed cing //mkhas pas hUM ni sgrogs bzhin pa//
32a1(rgyud): @#//blo ldan bum pa'i nang du bsam//de nas ral gri 'dzin pa yi//skyes pa bzhi po gra bcu chen po//khro bo gzhan gyi sems dang ldan//'jigs chen skyes bu de rnams kyis//
32a2(rgyud): ral gris nam mkha' bstun cing gsod//sangs rgyas thod pa'i sbyor ba dang //'di lta bu yi sbyor ldan pas//de bzhin du ni bud med bkru//skad cig de la bkrus tsam gyis//bud med bu dang ldan 
32a3(rgyud): par 'gyur//sangs rgyas thod pa zhes bya ba rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud bla na med pa gsang ba'i yang shin tu gsang ba las sman chen po khyad par can dang /mo sham gyi khrus kyi cho ga'i le'u ste bcu pa'o//
32a4(rgyud): de nas yang dag rab bshad pa//mkha' lding sbyor las byung ba'i le'u//sngon du gsungs pa'i cho ga yis//mkhas pas dkyil 'khor bri bar bya//blo ldan he ru ka dang ni//kun nas rnal 
32a5(rgyud): 'byor ma brgyad bskyed//mtshan ma phyag sogs sngon gsung bzhin//kha dog dkar po 'ba' zhig nyid//phyogs kyi rnal 'byor ma dkar zhing //mtshams kyi dmar po nyid yin no//
32a6(rgyud): sngon du gsungs pa'i cho g.yis//mkhas pas dkyil 'khaur byin gyis brlabs//bum pa rnam pa bzhi po la//sngon gsungs phyag mtshan bri bar bya//rig pa 'dzin pa'i sbyor ba 
32a7(rgyud): yis//phyis ni slob ma rjes su bzung //sngon gyi cho gas gsol btab te//sbyin dang tshogs kyi 'khor lo dang //de nas dkyil 'khor mchod par bya//slar yang gsang sngags 'di 
32b1(rgyud): yas ni//slob ma rjes su bzung bar bya//sa stengs na ni dus zin la//cung zad gso bar mi nus te//nam mkha' lding can gyis btang zhing //dus su zin ces smra ba na//sangs rgyas thod pa'i 
32b2(rgyud): rnal 'byor pa//blo ldan de nas las brtsam mo//bdag nyid he ru kar gyur cing //kun nas rnal 'byor ma brgyad de//de kun sku ni dkar po ru//cho ga bzhin du sbyar bar bya//'bras brtags rang 
32b3(rgyud): bzhin blangs nas ni//rig pa 'dzin pa gnyis bya'o//shin tu mnyam par gzhag pa yis//de yi lag par ral gri sbyin//de yi snying kar sngags bris gzhug/mdab ma gang dang gang de la//
32b4(rgyud): mar khu che byas snag tsha dang //mkhas pas sngags ni bri bar bya//oM bud+d+ha ka pA li ni hrIHoM ka pA li ni badzri Ni aHbi d+h+waM sa ya sarba nA ga na u da pa ya d+ya d+ha ra Ni/bi b+ha ya ka la da sha na/

33a2(rgyud): gsang ba'i yang shin tu gsang ba las rig pa bzhin sbyor ba'i man ngag nges pa'i le'u ste bcu gcig pa'o////de nas phyag na rdo rjes rnal 'byor ma sna tshogs snga ma la yang dang yang

34a2(rgyud): g.yon tu//dung chen mchog ni bzhag par bya//sbyor ba la ni mkhas pa yis//phyis ni pags kyis bya ba bya//padam las byung rdzas gang zhig/de nyid kyis ni dud pa mnye//ming med

35a1(rgyud): @#//shes par bya//de nas yang ngag rab bshad de//sbyor ba shin tu rnyed dka' ba//sngags dang rus pa'i sbyor ba yis//rtag tu sna tshogs las byed do//glang po'i rus

36a1(rgyud): @#//'gyur /zhe sdang g.yung mo nyid yin no//'dod chags khrus mkhan ma yin te//nga rgyal gtum mo nyid yin no//bra dog ko ba byed ma'o//snga bo de rnams bud med
